  126: function asStringForCategoryDerivation(value: unknown): string | null {
  127:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
  128:     return null;
  129:   }

  131:   const trimmed = value.trim();
  132:   return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : null;
  133: }

  135: function asNumberForCategoryDerivation(value: unknown): number | null {
  136:   if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
  137:     return value;
  138:   }
  139: 
  140:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  141:     const parsed = Number.parseFloat(value.trim().replace(",", "."));
  142:     return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : null;
  143:   }
  144: 
  145:   return null;
  146: }
  147:

  149:   if (typeof value === "boolean") {
  150:     return value;

  153:   return null;
  154: }

  421: async function runCategoryDerivationForCompleteRoute(params: {
  422:   event: ActivityEventRow;
  423:   completedStatus: string;

  425:   const policy = getCategoryDerivationPolicyForCompleteRoute(params);
  426:

  436:       resolutionCandidateCount: 0,
  437:       resolutionUnresolvedCount: 0,
  438:       persistenceDerivationRowsCreated: 0,
  439:       additionalCategoryLinks: [],
  440:       warnings: [],
  441:       errors: [],
  442:       policy,
  443:     };
  444:   }

  446:   try {
  447:     const { event } = params;
  448:

  450:       activityEventId: event.id,

  457:       organizationId: null,

  462:       },
  463:     };

  452:       title: event.title,

  570: function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  571:   if (typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value)) {
  572:     return value as Record<string, unknown>;
  573:   }
  574: 
  575:   return {};
  576: }
  577: 
  578: function asString(value: unknown): string | null {
  579:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
  580:     return null;
  581:   }
  582: 
  583:   const trimmed = value.trim();
  584: 
  585:   return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : null;
  586: }
  587: 
  588: function asNumber(value: unknown): number | null {
  589:   if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
  590:     return value;
  591:   }
  592: 
  593:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  594:     const normalized = value.trim().replace(",", ".");
  595:     const parsed = Number.parseFloat(normalized);
  596: 
  597:     if (Number.isFinite(parsed)) {
  598:       return parsed;
  599:     }
  600:   }
  601: 
  602:   return null;
  603: }

  605: function resolveCompletionTiming(params: {

  334: function buildAdditionalCategoryLinksForCompleteRoute(params: {
  335:   enabled: boolean;
  336:   activityEventId: string;
  337:   derivationRunId: string | null;
  338:   resolutionResult: unknown;
  339: }): AdditionalValueObjectCategoryLink[] {
  340:   const { enabled, activityEventId, derivationRunId, resolutionResult } = params;
  341: 
  342:   if (!enabled) {
  343:     return [];
  344:   }
  345: 
  346:   const resolutionRecord = asRecordForCategoryDerivation(resolutionResult);
  347:   const possibleCandidates = resolutionRecord.candidates;
  348:   const resolvedCandidates = Array.isArray(possibleCandidates)
  349:     ? possibleCandidates
  350:     : [];
  351: 
  352:   const allowedResolutionStatuses = new Set([
  353:     "resolved_existing",
  354:     "created_suggested",
  355:     "created_active",
  356:   ]);
  357: 
  358:   const links: AdditionalValueObjectCategoryLink[] = [];
  359: 
  360:   for (const candidate of resolvedCandidates) {
  361:     const categoryId = readStringFieldForCategoryDerivation(candidate, [
  362:       "categoryId",
  363:       "category_id",
  364:       "resolvedCategoryId",
  365:       "resolved_category_id",
  366:     ]);
  367: 
  368:     if (!isUuidLikeForCategoryDerivation(categoryId)) {
  369:       continue;
  370:     }
  371: 
  372:     const resolutionStatus =
  373:       readStringFieldForCategoryDerivation(candidate, [
  374:         "resolutionStatus",
  375:         "resolution_status",
  376:       ]) ?? "resolved_existing";
  377: 
  378:     if (!allowedResolutionStatuses.has(resolutionStatus)) {
  379:       continue;
  380:     }
  381: 
  382:     const candidateSlug =
  383:       readStringFieldForCategoryDerivation(candidate, [
  384:         "candidateSlug",
  385:         "candidate_slug",
  386:         "categorySlug",
  387:         "category_slug",
  388:         "slug",
  389:       ]) ?? categoryId;
  390: 
  391:     links.push({
  392:       categoryId,
  393:       categoryTable: "contextual_categories",
  394:       categoryRole: "semantic_component",
  395:       source: "rule",
  396:       confidence: readNumberFieldForCategoryDerivation(candidate, [
  397:         "confidence",
  398:         "score",
  399:       ]),
  400:       derivationRunId,
  401:       activityCategoryDerivationId: null,
  402:       activityEventId,
  403:       candidateSlug,
  404:       candidateTitle: readStringFieldForCategoryDerivation(candidate, [
  405:         "candidateTitle",
  406:         "candidate_title",
  407:         "title",
  408:         "label",
  409:         "name",
  410:       ]),
  411:       semanticLayer: readStringFieldForCategoryDerivation(candidate, [
  412:         "semanticLayer",
  413:         "semantic_layer",
  414:       ]),
  415:     });
  416:   }
  417: 
  418:   return links;
  419: }

  483:         derivationResult: extractionResult,

  511:       enabled: true,
  512:       activityEventId: event.id,
  513:       derivationRunId,
  514:       resolutionResult,
  515:     });

  518:       ...extractionResult.warnings,
  519:       ...resolutionResult.warnings,
  520:       ...readStringArrayFieldForCategoryDerivation(persistenceResult, ["warnings"]),
  521:     ];
  522: 
  523:     const errors = [
  524:       ...extractionResult.errors,
  525:       ...resolutionResult.errors,
  526:       ...readStringArrayFieldForCategoryDerivation(persistenceResult, ["errors"]),
  527:     ];

  532:       enabled: true,

  542:       additionalCategoryLinks,

  544:       errors,
  545:       policy,
  546:     };

  548:     const message =
  549:       error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown Category Derivation error";
  550: 
  551:     return {

  553:       ok: false,

  566:     };

  570: function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  571:   if (typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value)) {
  572:     return value as Record<string, unknown>;
  573:   }
  574: 
  575:   return {};
  576: }
  577: 
  578: function asString(value: unknown): string | null {
  579:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
  580:     return null;
  581:   }
  582: 
  583:   const trimmed = value.trim();
  584: 
  585:   return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : null;
  586: }
  587: 
  588: function asNumber(value: unknown): number | null {
  589:   if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
  590:     return value;
  591:   }
  592: 
  593:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  594:     const normalized = value.trim().replace(",", ".");
  595:     const parsed = Number.parseFloat(normalized);
  596: 
  597:     if (Number.isFinite(parsed)) {
  598:       return parsed;
  599:     }
  600:   }
  601: 
  602:   return null;
  603: }

  578: function asString(value: unknown): string | null {
  579:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
  580:     return null;
  581:   }
  582: 
  583:   const trimmed = value.trim();
  584: 
  585:   return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : null;
  586: }
  587: 
  588: function asNumber(value: unknown): number | null {
  589:   if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
  590:     return value;
  591:   }
  592: 
  593:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  594:     const normalized = value.trim().replace(",", ".");
  595:     const parsed = Number.parseFloat(normalized);
  596: 
  597:     if (Number.isFinite(parsed)) {
  598:       return parsed;
  599:     }
  600:   }
  601: 
  602:   return null;
  603: }

 1471:       .from("activity_events")
 1472:       .update({
 1473:         processing_status: "failed",
 1474:         updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
 1475:       })
 1476:       .eq("id", updatedEvent.id)
 1477:       .eq("user_id", appUser.id);
 1478: 
 1479:     return NextResponse.json(
 1480:       {
 1481:         ok: false,
 1482:         error:
 1483:           error instanceof Error
 1484:             ? error.message
 1485:             : "Failed to process rule-based activity impacts after completion.",
 1486:         event: updatedEvent,
 1487:       },
 1488:       { status: 500 }
 1489:     );
 1490:   }
 1491: }
